For small N, P > 1 splits the j-range into\n// P interleaved partial sums that are combined with a fixed shuffle tree so that\n// enough threads exist to fill the GPU.\ntemplate <int BS, int P>\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BS) stepKernel(const double* __restrict__ posIn, double* __restrict__ posOut,\n                                                 double* __restrict__ vel, int n, int lo, int hi) {\n    __shared__ double sh[3 * BS];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = tid % P;\n    const int i = lo + blockIdx.x * (BS / P) + tid / P;\n    const bool active = i < hi;\n\n    double px = 0.0, py = 0.0, pz = 0.0;\n    if (active) {\n        px = posIn[3 * (size_t)i + 0];\n        py = posIn[3 * (size_t)i + 1];\n        pz = posIn[3 * (size_t)i + 2];\n    }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int base = 0; base < n; base += BS) {\n        const int cnt = min(BS, n - base);\n        const double* src = posIn + 3 * (size_t)base;\n        for (int k = tid; k < 3 * cnt; k += BS) sh[k] = src[k];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (cnt == BS) {\n#pragma unroll 16\n            for (int j = lane; j < BS; j += P) {\n                const double dx = sh[3 * j + 0] - px;\n                const double dy = sh[3 * j + 1] - py;\n                const double dz = sh[3 * j + 2] - pz;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = lane; j < cnt; j += P) {\n                const double dx = sh[3 * j + 0] - px;\n                const double dy = sh[3 * j + 1] - py;\n                const double dz = sh[3 * j + 2] - pz;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if constexpr (P > 1) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int off = P / 2; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            Fx += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, Fx, off, P);\n            Fy += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, Fy, off, P);\n            Fz += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, Fz, off, P);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active && lane == 0) {\n        double vx = vel[3 * (size_t)i + 0];\n        double vy = vel[3 * (size_t)i + 1];\n        double vz = vel[3 * (size_t)i + 2];\n        vx += DT * Fx;\n        vy += DT * Fy;\n        vz += DT * Fz;\n        vel[3 * (size_t)i + 0] = vx;\n        vel[3 * (size_t)i + 1] = vy;\n        vel[3 * (size_t)i + 2] = vz;\n        px += vx * DT;\n        py += vy * DT;\n        pz += vz * DT;\n        posOut[3 * (size_t)i + 0] = px;\n        posOut[3 * (size_t)i + 1] = py;\n        posOut[3 * (size_t)i + 2] = pz;\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <int P>\nvoid launchStep(const double* posIn, double* posOut, double* vel, int n, int lo, int hi, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    constexpr int bodiesPerBlock = FORCE_BLOCK / P;\n    const int grid = (hi - lo + bodiesPerBlock - 1) / bodiesPerBlock;\n    stepKernel<FORCE_BLOCK, P><<<grid, FORCE_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(posIn, posOut, vel, n, lo, hi);\n}\n\nvoid launchStep(int p, const double* posIn, double* posOut, double* vel, int n, int lo, int hi,\n                cudaStream_t stream) {\n    switch (p) {\n        case 1: launchStep<1>(posIn, posOut, vel, n, lo, hi, stream); off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            Fx += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, Fx, off, P);\n            Fy += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, Fy, off, P);\n            Fz += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, Fz, off, P);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active && lane == 0) {\n        double vx = vel[3 * (size_t)i + 0];\n        double vy = vel[3 * (size_t)i + 1];\n        double vz = vel[3 * (size_t)i + 2];\n        vx += DT * Fx;\n        vy += DT * Fy;\n        vz += DT * Fz;\n        vel[3 * (size_t)i + 0] = vx;\n        vel[3 * (size_t)i + 1] = vy;\n        vel[3 * (size_t)i + 2] = vz;\n        px += vx * DT;\n        py += vy * DT;\n        pz += vz * DT;\n        posOut[3 * (size_t)i + 0] = px;\n        posOut[3 * (size_t)i + 1] = py;\n        posOut[3 * (size_t)i + 2] = pz;\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <int P>\nvoid launchStep(const double* posIn, double* posOut, double* vel, int n, int lo, int hi, cudaStream_t stream) {\n    constexpr int bodiesPerBlock = FORCE_BLOCK / P;\n    const int grid = (hi - lo + bodiesPerBlock - 1) / bodiesPerBlock;\n    stepKernel<FORCE_BLOCK, P><<<grid, FORCE_BLOCK, 0, stream>>>(posIn, posOut, vel, n, lo, hi);\n}\n\nvoid launchStep(int p, const double* posIn, double* posOut, double* vel, int n, int lo, int hi,\n                cudaStream_t stream) {\n    switch (p) {\n        case 1: launchStep<1>(posIn, posOut, vel, n, lo, hi, stream); {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfh4E6RRkp4e5M4hFY71M","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The nbody benchmark now runs entirely on the GPU with CUDA. It builds cleanly with no warnings, and the executable is still `nbody`. Only `nbody.cpp` and `CMakeLists.txt` changed, and the temporary build directory was removed.\n\n**Speed (original CPU vs. CUDA, measured here on 4× RTX 3090):**\n\n| Bodies | Steps | Original | CUDA |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1,024 (default) | 10 | 77 ms | 2 ms |\n| 16,384 | 10 | 19.9 s | ~150 ms |\n| 65,536 | 1 | 31.9 s | 113 ms |\n\nUsing several GPUs scales well: at 32,768 bodies, 4 GPUs are 3.5× faster than 1.\n\n**Accuracy:** the outputs don't match the original bit for bit, and can't. The simulation is chaotic, so tiny rounding differences grow quickly. Even recompiling the original on the CPU with FMA turned off changes its output hash. Against the original, the CUDA version agrees to about 1e-12 relative at the default size, which is the same size of difference FMA causes. Results with 0, 1 and 3 bodies match exactly, and validation passes at every size I tried.\n\n**How it works:**\n- **One kernel per step:** it computes the forces and moves the bodies together. Old and new positions are kept in separate buffers, so the result is the same as the original's two-pass approach.\n- **Same arithmetic as the original:** double precision, IEEE-exact `1.0/sqrt`, and, for large inputs, one thread per body adding up forces in the original order.\n- **Small inputs:** when there are too few bodies to keep the GPU busy, several threads share each body and their partial sums are combined in a fixed order. The result is still the same every run, but rounds slightly differently. This is what took the default case from 11 ms to 2 ms.\n- **Multiple GPUs:** once every visible GPU would get at least 1,024 bodies, the bodies are split across all of them. These GPUs can't talk to each other directly, so after each step each one shares its new positions with the others through pinned host memory.\n- **Energy check (`-v`):** also computed on the GPU, which adds up the terms in a different order. The printed energy can differ from the original in the last digits; pass/fail doesn't depend on it.\n\n**Things to know:**\n- Results depend a little on how many GPUs are visible, because that changes how the work is divided. They are the same from run to run on the same setup.\n- The timed section now also includes copying data to and from the GPU, which makes the comparison fair. GPU setup and memory allocation happen before the timer starts.\n- `CMakeLists.txt` now compiles `nbody.cpp` as CUDA and targets the GPU in the build machine (`CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`). It requires CMake 3.24 or newer and defaults to a Release build."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":579,"cache_read_input_tokens":43261,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":579},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fe5d4c9e-6c28-4999-998d-b659322a87f4","uuid":"0d15a4a8-2737-414f-b81d-c982c8c1c59f","timestamp":"2026-10-04T10:41:39.533Z","request_id":"req_011Cfh4E5tRj7mTqaFYQxhkM"}
